This skill covers extracting audio from video files for speech processing tasks.\n\n## Installation\nFFmpeg is typically available via system package managers:\n```bash\n# Ubuntu/Debian\nsudo apt-get install ffmpeg\n\n# macOS\nbrew install ffmpeg\n```\n\n## Basic Audio Extraction\n\n### Extract audio to WAV format (recommended for speech processing)\n```bash\n# Extract audio as 16kHz mono WAV (optimal for speech models)\nffmpeg -i input.mp4 -vn -acodec pcm_s16le -ar 16000 -ac 1 output.wav\n\n# Parameters explained:\n# -i input.mp4    : Input file\n# -vn             : No video (audio only)\n# -acodec pcm_s16le : 16-bit PCM encoding\n# -ar 16000       : Sample rate 16kHz (standard for speech)\n# -ac 1           : Mono channel\n```\n\n### Get audio duration\n```bash\nffprobe -i input.mp4 -show_entries format=duration -v quiet -of csv=\"p=0\"\n```\n\n### Get detailed audio info\n```bash\nffprobe -i input.mp4 -show_streams -select_streams a -v quiet\n```\n\n## Python Integration\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport json\n\ndef extract_audio(input_file, output_file, sample_rate=16000):\n    \"\"\"Extract audio from video file.\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffmpeg', '-y',  # -y to overwrite\n        '-i', input_file,\n        '-vn',\n        '-acodec', 'pcm_s16le',\n        '-ar', str(sample_rate),\n        '-ac', '1',\n        output_file\n    ]\n    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)\n\ndef get_audio_duration(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio/video duration in seconds.\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffprobe', '-v', 'quiet',\n        '-show_entries', 'format=duration',\n        '-of', 'json',\n        file_path\n    ]\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\n    data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n    return float(data['format']['duration'])\n```\n\n## Use Cases\n- Pre-processing video for speech recognition\n- Extracting audio for diarization\n- Converting audio formats for ML pipelines\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":365,"cache_read_input_tokens":23128,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":365,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e4b133c-4bb3-4b31-a374-bbf20ad9a60e","uuid":"86cbb02d-1d2b-4709-b720-4a8303d1d257"}

Find speaker at segment midpoint\n        speaker = None\n        for turn, _, spk in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n            if turn.start <= seg_mid <= turn.end:\n                speaker = spk\n                break\n\n        aligned.append({\n            \"start\": seg_start,\n            \"end\": seg_end,\n            \"text\": segment[\"text\"].strip(),\n            \"speaker\": speaker or \"UNKNOWN\"\n        })\n\n    return aligned\n```\n\n## Output Formats\n\n### SRT Format\n```python\ndef to_srt(segments):\n    srt = []\n    for i, seg in enumerate(segments, 1):\n        start = format_timestamp(seg[\"start\"], srt=True)\n        end = format_timestamp(seg[\"end\"], srt=True)\n        srt.append(f\"{i}\\n{start} --> {end}\\n{seg['text']}\\n\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(srt)\n```\n\n### VTT Format\n```python\ndef to_vtt(segments):\n    vtt = [\"WEBVTT\\n\"]\n    for seg in segments:\n        start = format_timestamp(seg[\"start\"])\n        end = format_timestamp(seg[\"end\"])\n        vtt.append(f\"{start} --> {end}\\n{seg['text']}\\n\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(vtt)\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n- Use `fp16=False` on CPU\n- For memory issues, use smaller models\n- Ensure 16kHz sample rate for best results\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":365,"cache_read_input_tokens":23128,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":365,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e4b133c-4bb3-4b31-a374-bbf20ad9a60e","uuid":"7d1c9407-25a2-4291-91c6-0b74f22f3448"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01NHcBn5KjUZbUKxxcvtSj51","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_019aJP9JHoRNt1NCqrjPM9Fc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/ass-subtitle-format.md","content":"# ASS/SSA Subtitle Format\n\n## Overview\nASS (Advanced SubStation Alpha) is a subtitle format that supports advanced styling, positioning, and effects. It's commonly used for anime and videos requiring styled subtitles.\n\n## File Structure\n\n### Basic ASS File\n```\n[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\nDialogue: 0,0:00:00.00,0:00:05.00,Default,,0,0,0,,Hello World\n```\n\n## Time Format\nASS uses the format: `H:MM:SS.CC` (hours:minutes:seconds.centiseconds)\n- Centiseconds are 1/100th of a second\n- Example: `0:01:23.45` = 1 minute, 23 seconds, 450 milliseconds\n\n```python\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format (H:MM:SS.CC).\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centiseconds = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centiseconds:02d}\"\n```\n\n## Dialogue Line Format\n```\nDialogue: Layer,Start,End,Style,Name,MarginL,MarginR,MarginV,Effect,Text\n```\n\n| Field    | Description                          |\n|----------|--------------------------------------|\n| Layer    | Z-order (0 = bottom)                 |\n| Start    | Start time (H:MM:SS.CC)              |\n| End      | End time (H:MM:SS.CC)                |\n| Style    | Style name from [V4+ Styles]         |\n| Name     | Actor/speaker name (optional)        |\n| MarginL  | Left margin override (0 = default)   |\n| MarginR  | Right margin override                |\n| MarginV  | Vertical margin override             |\n| Effect   | Transition effect (optional)         |\n| Text     | The subtitle text                    |\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n### Generate ASS File\n```python\ndef create_ass_file(segments, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create ASS subtitle file from segments with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n\n    header = \"\"\"[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Speaker Diarization Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(header)\n        for seg in segments:\n            start = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['start'])\n            end = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['end'])\n            speaker = seg.get('speaker', 'SPEAKER_00')\n            text = seg['text'].replace('\\n', '\\\\N')  # ASS newline\n\n            # Format: SPEAKER_XX: It describes \"who spoke when\" in an audio file.\n\n## Format Specification\n\n### Line Format\n```\nSPEAKER <file_id> <channel> <start_time> <duration> <ortho> <stype> <speaker_id> <conf> <slat>\n```\n\n### Fields\n\n| Field      | Description                                        | Example    |\n|------------|---------------------------------------------------|------------|\n| Type       | Always \"SPEAKER\" for diarization                   | SPEAKER    |\n| file_id    | Audio file identifier (without extension)          | input      |\n| channel    | Audio channel (usually 1)                          | 1          |\n| start_time | Segment start time in seconds                      | 0.804000   |\n| duration   | Segment duration in seconds                        | 0.860000   |\n| ortho      | Orthography (usually \\<NA\\>)                       | \\<NA\\>     |\n| stype      | Speaker type (usually \\<NA\\>)                      | \\<NA\\>     |\n| speaker_id | Speaker identifier                                 | spk00      |\n| conf       | Confidence score (usually \\<NA\\>)                  | \\<NA\\>     |\n| slat       | Speaker latency (usually \\<NA\\>)                   | \\<NA\\>     |\n\n### Example RTTM File\n```\nSPEAKER input 1 0.804000 0.860000 <NA> <NA> spk00 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 2.100000 1.500000 <NA> <NA> spk01 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 4.200000 2.300000 <NA> <NA> spk00 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 7.800000 1.100000 <NA> <NA> spk01 <NA> <NA>\n```\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n### Writing RTTM from pyannote\n```python\n# Using file_id=\"input\"):\n    \"\"\"Write diarization results to RTTM format.\"\"\"\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        for segment, track, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n            start = segment.start\n            duration = segment.duration\n\n            # Normalize speaker ID (SPEAKER_0 -> spk00)\n            if speaker.startswith(\"SPEAKER_\"):\n                spk_num = int(speaker.split(\"_\")[1])\n                spk_id = f\"spk{spk_num:02d}\"\n            else:\n                spk_id = speaker\n\n            line = f\"SPEAKER {file_id} 1 {start:.6f} {duration:.6f} <NA> <NA> {spk_id} <NA> <NA}\\n\"\n            f.write(line)\n```\n\n### Reading RTTM\n```python\ndef read_rttm(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Parse RTTM file into list of segments.\"\"\"\n    segments = []\n    with open(rttm_path, 'r') as f:\n        for line in f:\n            parts = line.strip().split()\n            if len(parts) float(parts[3]),\n                    'duration': float(parts[4]),\n                    'speaker': parts[7]\n                })\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Convert RTTM to Segments\n```python\ndef rttm_to_segments(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Convert RTTM to segment list with start/end times.\"\"\"\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n    return [\n        {\n            'start': seg['start'],\n            'end': seg['start'] + seg['duration'],\n            'speaker': seg['speaker']\n        }\n        for seg in segments\n    ]\n```\n\n## Statistics from RTTM\n\n```python\ndef rttm_statistics(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Calculate statistics from RTTM file.\"\"\"\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n\n    speakers = set(seg['speaker'] for seg in segments)\n    total_speech = sum(seg['duration'] for seg in segments)\n\n    speaker_times = {}\n    for seg in segments:\n        spk = seg['speaker']\n        speaker_times[spk] = speaker_times.get(spk, 0) + seg['duration']\n\n    return {\n        'num_speakers': len(speakers),\n        'total_speech_time': total_speech,\n        'speaker_times': speaker_times\n    }\n```\n\n## Integration with Pyannote\n\n```python\nfrom pyannote.core import Annotation, Segment\n\ndef rttm_to_annotation(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Convert RTTM to pyannote Annotation object.\"\"\"\n    annotation = Annotation()\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        annotation[Segment(seg['start'], seg['start'] + seg['duration'])] = seg['speaker']\n\n    return annotation\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Times are in seconds with 6 decimal precision\n- Speaker IDs typically use format `spkXX` or `SPEAKER_XX`\n- Channel is usually 1 for mono audio\n- Most fields use This skill covers extracting audio from video files for speech processing tasks.\n\n## Installation\nFFmpeg is typically available via system package managers:\n```bash\n# Ubuntu/Debian\nsudo apt-get install ffmpeg\n\n# macOS\nbrew install ffmpeg\n```\n\n## Basic Audio Extraction\n\n### Extract audio to WAV format (recommended for speech processing)\n```bash\n# Extract audio as 16kHz mono WAV (optimal for speech models)\nffmpeg -i input.mp4 -vn -acodec pcm_s16le -ar 16000 -ac 1 output.wav\n\n# Parameters explained:\n# -i input.mp4    : Input file\n# -vn             : No video (audio only)\n# -acodec pcm_s16le : 16-bit PCM encoding\n# -ar 16000       : Sample rate 16kHz (standard for speech)\n# -ac 1           : Mono channel\n```\n\n### Get audio duration\n```bash\nffprobe -i input.mp4 -show_entries format=duration -v quiet -of csv=\"p=0\"\n```\n\n### Get detailed audio info\n```bash\nffprobe -i input.mp4 -show_streams -select_streams a -v quiet\n```\n\n## Python Integration\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport json\n\ndef extract_audio(input_file, output_file, sample_rate=16000):\n    \"\"\"Extract audio from video file.\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffmpeg', '-y',  # -y to overwrite\n        '-i', input_file,\n        '-vn',\n        '-acodec', 'pcm_s16le',\n        '-ar', str(sample_rate),\n        '-ac', '1',\n        output_file\n    ]\n    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)\n\ndef get_audio_duration(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio/video duration in seconds.\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffprobe', '-v', 'quiet',\n        '-show_entries', 'format=duration',\n        '-of', 'json',\n        file_path\n    ]\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\n    data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n    return float(data['format']['duration'])\n```\n\n## Use Cases\n- Pre-processing video for speech recognition\n- Extracting audio for diarization\n- Converting audio formats for ML pipelines\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

It's commonly used for anime and videos requiring styled subtitles.\n\n## File Structure\n\n### Basic ASS File\n```\n[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\nDialogue: 0,0:00:00.00,0:00:05.00,Default,,0,0,0,,Hello World\n```\n\n## Time Format\nASS uses the format: `H:MM:SS.CC` (hours:minutes:seconds.centiseconds)\n- Centiseconds are 1/100th of a second\n- Example: `0:01:23.45` = 1 minute, 23 seconds, 450 milliseconds\n\n```python\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format (H:MM:SS.CC).\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centiseconds = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centiseconds:02d}\"\n```\n\n## Dialogue Line Format\n```\nDialogue: Layer,Start,End,Style,Name,MarginL,MarginR,MarginV,Effect,Text\n```\n\n| Field    | Description                          |\n|----------|--------------------------------------|\n| Layer    | Z-order (0 = bottom)                 |\n| Start    | Start time (H:MM:SS.CC)              |\n| End      | End time (H:MM:SS.CC)                |\n| Style    | Style name from [V4+ Styles]         |\n| Name     | Actor/speaker name (optional)        |\n| MarginL  | Left margin override (0 = default)   |\n| MarginR  | Right margin override                |\n| MarginV  | Vertical margin override             |\n| Effect   | Transition effect (optional)         |\n| Text     | The subtitle text                    |\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n### Generate ASS File\n```python\ndef create_ass_file(segments, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create ASS subtitle file from segments with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n\n    header = \"\"\"[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Speaker Diarization Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(header)\n        for seg in segments:\n            start = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['start'])\n            end = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['end'])\n            speaker = seg.get('speaker', 'SPEAKER_00')\n            text = seg['text'].replace('\\n', '\\\\N')  # ASS newline\n\n            # Format: SPEAKER_XX: transcript\n            line = f\"Dialogue: 0,{start},{end},Default,,0,0,0,,{speaker}: {text}\\n\"\n            f.write(line)\n\n\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format.\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centiseconds = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centiseconds:02d}\"\n```\n\n### Styling Options\n\n#### Color Format\nColors in ASS are in `&HAABBGGRR` format (Alpha, Blue, Green, Red):\n- `&H00FFFFFF` = White (fully opaque)\n- `&H000000FF` = Red\n- `&H00FF0000` = Blue\n- `&H0000FF00` = Green\n\n#### Override Tags\n```\n{\\b1}Bold text{\\b0}\n{\\i1}Italic text{\\i0}\n{\\u1}Underline{\\u0}\n{\\c&H0000FF&}Red text{\\c}\n{\\fs24}Larger font{\\fs}\n{\\pos(100,200)}Position override\n```\n\n## Integration with Diarization\n```python\ndef create_diarized_subtitles(transcription_segments, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create ASS file with speaker-labeled transcription.\"\"\"\n\n    # Convert speaker labels to SPEAKER_XX format\n    for seg in transcription_segments:\n        if 'speaker' in seg:\n            # Normalize speaker label (e.g., \"SPEAKER_0\" -> \"SPEAKER_00\")\n            spk = seg['speaker']\n            if spk.startswith('SPEAKER_'):\n                num = int(spk.split('_')[1])\n It describes \"who spoke when\" in an audio file.\n\n## Format Specification\n\n### Line Format\n```\nSPEAKER <file_id> <channel> <start_time> <duration> <ortho> <stype> <speaker_id> <conf> <slat>\n```\n\n### Fields\n\n| Field      | Description                                        | Example    |\n|------------|---------------------------------------------------|------------|\n| Type       | Always \"SPEAKER\" for diarization                   | SPEAKER    |\n| file_id    | Audio file identifier (without extension)          | input      |\n| channel    | Audio channel (usually 1)                          | 1          |\n| start_time | Segment start time in seconds                      | 0.804000   |\n| duration   | Segment duration in seconds                        | 0.860000   |\n| ortho      | Orthography (usually \\<NA\\>)                       | \\<NA\\>     |\n| stype      | Speaker type (usually \\<NA\\>)                      | \\<NA\\>     |\n| speaker_id | Speaker identifier                                 | spk00      |\n| conf       | Confidence score (usually \\<NA\\>)                  | \\<NA\\>     |\n| slat       | Speaker latency (usually \\<NA\\>)                   | \\<NA\\>     |\n\n### Example RTTM File\n```\nSPEAKER input 1 0.804000 0.860000 <NA> <NA> spk00 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 2.100000 1.500000 <NA> <NA> spk01 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 4.200000 2.300000 <NA> <NA> spk00 <NA> <NA>\nSPEAKER input 1 7.800000 1.100000 <NA> <NA> spk01 <NA> <NA>\n```\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n### Writing RTTM from pyannote\n```python\n# Using pyannote's built-in method\nwith open(\"output.rttm\", \"w\") as f:\n    diarization.write_rttm(f)\n```\n\n### Custom RTTM Writer\n```python\ndef write_rttm(diarization, output_path, file_id=\"input\"):\n    \"\"\"Write diarization results to RTTM format.\"\"\"\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        for segment, track, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n            start = segment.start\n            duration = segment.duration\n\n            # Normalize speaker ID (SPEAKER_0 -> spk00)\n            if speaker.startswith(\"SPEAKER_\"):\n                spk_num = int(speaker.split(\"_\")[1])\n                spk_id = f\"spk{spk_num:02d}\"\n            else:\n                spk_id = speaker\n\n            line = f\"SPEAKER {file_id} 1 {start:.6f} {duration:.6f} <NA> <NA> {spk_id} <NA> <NA}\\n\"\n            f.write(line)\n```\n\n### Reading RTTM\n```python\ndef read_rttm(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Parse RTTM file into list of segments.\"\"\"\n    segments = []\n    with open(rttm_path, 'r') as f:\n        for line in f:\n            parts = line.strip().split()\n            if len(parts) >= 8 and parts[0] == \"SPEAKER\":\n                segments.append({\n                    'file_id': parts[1],\n                    'channel': int(parts[2]),\n                    'start': float(parts[3]),\n                    'duration': float(parts[4]),\n                    'speaker': parts[7]\n                })\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Convert RTTM to Segments\n```python\ndef rttm_to_segments(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Convert RTTM to segment list with start/end times.\"\"\"\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n    return [\n        {\n            'start': seg['start'],\n            'end': seg['start'] + seg['duration'],\n            'speaker': seg['speaker']\n        }\n        for seg in segments\n    ]\n```\n\n## Statistics from RTTM\n\n```python\ndef rttm_statistics(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Calculate statistics from RTTM file.\"\"\"\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n\n    speakers = set(seg['speaker'] for seg in segments)\n    total_speech = sum(seg['duration'] for seg in segments)\n\n    speaker_times = {}\n    for seg in segments:\n        spk = seg['speaker']\n        speaker_times[spk] = speaker_times.get(spk, 0) + seg['duration']\n\n    return {\n        'num_speakers': len(speakers),\n        'total_speech_time': total_speech,\n        'speaker_times': speaker_times\n    }\n```\n\n## Integration with Pyannote\n\n```python\nfrom pyannote.core import Annotation, Segment\n\ndef rttm_to_annotation(rttm_path):\n    \"\"\"Convert RTTM to pyannote Annotation object.\"\"\"\n    annotation = Annotation()\n    segments = read_rttm(rttm_path)\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        annotation[Segment(seg['start'], seg['start'] + seg['duration'])] = seg['speaker']\n\n    return annotation\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Times are in seconds with 6 decimal precision\n- Speaker IDs typically use format `spkXX` or `SPEAKER_XX`\n- Channel is usually 1 for mono audio\n- Most fields use `<NA>` as placeholder values\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

ASS newline escape\n\n            f.write(f\"Dialogue: 0,{start},{end},Default,,0,0,0,,{speaker}: {text}\\n\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    rttm_path = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    ass_path = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n    report_path = \"/root/report.json\"\n\n    # Step 2: Voice Activity Detection\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Performing Voice Activity Detection...\")\n    vad_segments = perform_vad_simple(waveform, sr)\n    print(f\"  Found {len(vad_segments)} speech segments\")\n    report[\"steps_completed\"].append(\"voice_activity_detection\")\n    report[\"tools_used\"][\"vad\"] = \"energy-based VAD\"\n\n    # Step 3: Speaker Diarization\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Performing Speaker Diarization...\")\n    print(\"  Loading SpeechBrain speaker model...\")\n    speaker_model = SpeakerRecognition.from_hparams(\n        source=\"speechbrain/spkrec-ecapa-voxceleb\",\n        savedir=\"/root/.cache/speechbrain/spkrec-ecapa-voxceleb\"\n    )\n    libraries.add(\"speechbrain\")\n\n    print(\"  Extracting speaker embeddings...\")\n    embeddings, valid_segments = extract_speaker_embeddings(waveform, sr, vad_segments, speaker_model)\n\n    if embeddings is not None and len(embeddings) > 0:\n        print(f\"  Extracted {len(embeddings)} embeddings\")\n\n        print(\"  Clustering speakers...\")\n        speaker_labels = cluster_speakers(embeddings)\n        num_speakers = len(set(speaker_labels))\n        print(f\"  Detected {num_speakers} speakers\")\n\n        # Assign speaker labels to segments\n        speaker_segments = []\n        for seg, label in zip(valid_segments, speaker_labels):\n            speaker_segments.append({\n                'start': seg['start'],\n                'end': seg['end'],\n                'speaker': f\"SPEAKER_{label:02d}\"\n            })\n\n        report[\"num_speakers_pred\"] = num_speakers\n    else:\n        print(\"  No valid speech segments found for embedding extraction\")\n        speaker_segments = []\n        for seg in vad_segments:\n            speaker_segments.append({\n                'start': seg['start'],\n                'end': seg['end'],\n                'speaker': \"SPEAKER_00\"\n            })\n        report[\"num_speakers_pred\"] = 1\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01WEykpEJHsyHctdj2xm3rhn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01X46yjG6TjAkbjT7F5CJsLi","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/diarize_transcribe.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSpeaker Diarization and Transcription Script\nUses audio feature extraction and clustering for diarization, Whisper for transcription.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport json\nimport warnings\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Suppress warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\")\nos.environ[\"TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM\"] = \"false\"\n\nimport torch\nimport torchaudio\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, fcluster\nfrom scipy.spatial.distance import pdist\nimport whisper\n\n\ndef seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format (H:MM:SS.CC).\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centiseconds = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centiseconds:02d}\"\n\n\ndef get_audio_duration(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio duration in seconds.\"\"\"\n    info = torchaudio.info(audio_path)\n    return info.num_frames / info.sample_rate\n\n\ndef load_audio(audio_path, target_sr=16000):\n    \"\"\"Load audio file and resample if needed.\"\"\"\n    waveform, sr = torchaudio.load(audio_path)\n    if sr != target_sr:\n        resampler = torchaudio.transforms.Resample(sr, target_sr)\n        waveform = resampler(waveform)\n    # Convert to mono if stereo\n    if waveform.shape[0] > 1:\n        waveform = waveform.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)\n    return waveform, target_sr\n\n\ndef extract_mfcc_features(waveform, sr, n_mfcc=20):\n    \"\"\"Extract MFCC features from audio.\"\"\"\n    mfcc_transform = torchaudio.transforms.MFCC(\n        sample_rate=sr,\n        n_mfcc=n_mfcc,\n        melkwargs={'n_fft': 400, 'hop_length': 160, 'n_mels': 40}\n    )\n    mfccs = mfcc_transform(waveform)\n    return mfccs\n\n\ndef perform_vad_with_whisper(whisper_result):\n    \"\"\"Extract speech segments from Whisper transcription.\"\"\"\n    segments = []\n    for seg in whisper_result[\"segments\"]:\n        segments.append({\n            'start': seg['start'],\n            'end': seg['end'],\n            'text': seg['text'].strip()\n        })\n    return segments\n\n\ndef extract_segment_features(waveform, sr, segments, n_mfcc=20):\n    \"\"\"Extract MFCC-based features for each segment.\"\"\"\n    mfcc_transform = torchaudio.transforms.MFCC(\n        sample_rate=sr,\n        n_mfcc=n_mfcc,\n        melkwargs={'n_fft': 400, 'hop_length': 160, 'n_mels': 40}\n    )\n\n    features = []\n    valid_segments = []\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        start_sample = int(seg['start'] * sr)\n        end_sample = int(seg['end'] * sr)\n\n        # Ensure minimum length\n        if end_sample - start_sample < sr * 0.3:  # Min 300ms\n            continue\n\n        segment_audio = waveform[:, start_sample:end_sample]\n\n        # Extract MFCCs\n        mfccs = mfcc_transform(segment_audio)\n\n        # Compute mean and std across time for each MFCC coefficient\n        mfcc_mean = mfccs.mean(dim=2).squeeze().numpy()\n        mfcc_std = mfccs.std(dim=2).squeeze().numpy()\n\n        # Combine mean and std as feature vector\n        feature_vector = np.concatenate([mfcc_mean, mfcc_std])\n        features.append(feature_vector)\n        valid_segments.append(seg)\n\n    return np.array(features) if features else None, valid_segments\n\n\ndef cluster_speakers(embeddings, threshold=1.0):\n    \"\"\"Cluster speaker embeddings using hierarchical clustering.\"\"\"\n    if embeddings is None or len(embeddings) < 2:\n        return [0] * (len(embeddings) if embeddings is not None else 0)\n\n    # Normalize features\n    from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    embeddings_norm = scaler.fit_transform(embeddings)\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances\n    distances = pdist(embeddings_norm, metric='euclidean')\n\n    # Hierarchical clustering\n    linkage_matrix = linkage(distances, method='ward')\n\n    # Use dynamic threshold based on distance distribution\n    labels = fcluster(linkage_matrix, threshold, criterion='distance')\n\n    # Convert to 0-indexed\n    return [l - 1 for l in labels]\n\n\ndef transcribe_with_whisper(audio_path, model_name=\"base\"):\n    \"\"\"Transcribe audio using Whisper with word timestamps.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading Whisper model '{model_name}'...\")\n    model = whisper.load_model(model_name)\n\n    print(\"Transcribing audio...\")\n    result = model.transcribe(\n        audio_path,\n        word_timestamps=True,\n        fp16=False,  # CPU mode\n        verbose=False\n    )\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef assign_speakers_to_segments(segments, speaker_labels):\n    \"\"\"Assign speaker labels to segments.\"\"\"\n    labeled_segments = []\n    for seg, label in zip(segments, speaker_labels):\n        labeled_segments.append({\n            'start': seg['start'],\n            'end': seg['end'],\n            'text': seg.get('text', ''),\n            'speaker': f\"SPEAKER_{label:02d}\"\n        })\n    return labeled_segments\n\n\ndef merge_consecutive_segments(segments):\n    \"\"\"Merge consecutive segments with the same speaker.\"\"\"\n    if not segments:\n        return []\n\n    merged = [segments[0].copy()]\n    for seg in segments[1:]:\n        if seg['speaker'] == merged[-1]['speaker'] and seg['start'] - merged[-1]['end'] < 1.0:\n            merged[-1]['end'] = seg['end']\n            merged[-1]['text'] = merged[-1]['text'] + ' ' + seg['text']\n        else:\n            merged.append(seg.copy())\n    return merged\n\n\ndef write_rttm(speaker_segments, output_path, file_id=\"input\"):\n    \"\"\"Write speaker diarization to RTTM format.\"\"\"\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        for seg in speaker_segments:\n            start = seg['start']\n            duration = seg['end'] - seg['start']\n            # Convert SPEAKER_XX to spkXX format\n            spk = seg['speaker']\n            if spk.startswith(\"SPEAKER_\"):\n                num = int(spk.split(\"_\")[1])\n                spk_id = f\"spk{num:02d}\"\n            else:\n                spk_id = spk\n\n            f.write(f\"SPEAKER {file_id} 1 {start:.6f} {duration:.6f} <NA> <NA> {spk_id} <NA> <NA>\\n\")\n\n\ndef write_ass_subtitles(aligned_segments, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Write ASS subtitle file with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n    header = \"\"\"[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Speaker Diarization Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(header)\n        for seg in aligned_segments:\n            start = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['start'])\n            end = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['end'])\n            speaker = seg['speaker']\n            text = seg['text'].replace('\\n', '\\\\N')  # ASS newline escape\n\n            f.write(f\"Dialogue: 0,{start},{end},Default,,0,0,0,,{speaker}: {text}\\n\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    rttm_path = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    ass_path = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n    report_path = \"/root/report.json\"\n\n    # Initialize report\n    report = {\n        \"num_speakers_pred\": 0,\n        \"total_speech_time_sec\": 0.0,\n        \"audio_duration_sec\": 0.0,\n        \"steps_completed\": [],\n        \"commands_used\": [\"python3\", \"ffmpeg\"],\n        \"libraries_used\": [],\n        \"tools_used\": {},\n        \"notes\": \"\"\n    }\n\n    libraries = set([\"torch\", \"torchaudio\", \"numpy\", \"scipy\", \"sklearn\"])\n\n    # Step 1: Load audio\n    print(\"Step 1: Loading audio...\")\n    audio_duration = get_audio_duration(audio_path)\n    waveform, sr = load_audio(audio_path)\n    report[\"audio_duration_sec\"] = round(audio_duration, 2)\n    print(f\"  Audio duration: {audio_duration:.2f}s\")\n    report[\"steps_completed\"].append(\"audio_extraction\")\n    report[\"tools_used\"][\"audio_extraction\"] = \"FFmpeg + torchaudio\"\n\n    # Get speech segments from Whisper\n    speech_segments = perform_vad_with_whisper(whisper_result)\n    print(f\"  Found {len(speech_segments)} speech segments\")\n    report[\"steps_completed\"].append(\"voice_activity_detection\")\n    report[\"tools_used\"][\"vad\"] = \"Whisper VAD\"\n\n    # Step 3: Extract features for speaker clustering\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Extracting audio features for speaker clustering...\")\n    features, valid_segments = extract_segment_features(waveform, sr, speech_segments)\n\n    if features is not None and len(features) > 1:\n        print(f\"  Extracted features for {len(features)} segments\")\n\n        # Step 4: Cluster speakers\n        print(\"\\nStep 4: Clustering speakers...\")\n        speaker_labels = cluster_speakers(features)\n        num_speakers = len(set(speaker_labels))\n        print(f\"  Detected {num_speakers} speaker(s)\")\n\n        # Assign speaker labels\n        labeled_segments = assign_speakers_to_segments(valid_segments, speaker_labels)\n    else:\n        print(\"  Not enough segments for clustering, assuming single speaker\")\n        labeled_segments = []\n        for seg in speech_segments:\n            labeled_segments.append({\n                'start': seg['start'],\n                'end': seg['end'],\n                'text': seg.get('text', ''),\n                'speaker': \"SPEAKER_00\"\n            })\n        num_speakers = 1\n\n    report[\"num_speakers_pred\"] = num_speakers\n    report[\"steps_completed\"].append(\"speaker_diarization\")\n    report[\"tools_used\"][\"diarization\"] = \"MFCC features + hierarchical clustering (ward linkage)\"\n\n    # seconds_to_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to ASS time format (H:MM:SS.CC).\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = int(seconds % 60)\n    centiseconds = int((seconds % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{secs:02d}.{centiseconds:02d}\"\n\n\ndef get_audio_duration(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio duration in seconds.\"\"\"\n    info = torchaudio.info(audio_path)\n    return info.num_frames / info.sample_rate\n\n\ndef load_audio(audio_path, target_sr=16000):\n    \"\"\"Load audio file and resample if needed.\"\"\"\n    waveform, sr = torchaudio.load(audio_path)\n    if sr != target_sr:\n        resampler = torchaudio.transforms.Resample(sr, target_sr)\n        waveform = resampler(waveform)\n    # Convert to mono if stereo\n    if waveform.shape[0] > 1:\n        waveform = waveform.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)\n    return waveform, target_sr\n\n\ndef perform_vad_simple(waveform, sr, frame_duration=0.03, energy_threshold=0.01):\n    \"\"\"Simple energy-based VAD to find speech segments.\"\"\"\n    frame_size = int(sr * frame_duration)\n    num_frames = waveform.shape[1] // frame_size\n\n    # Calculate frame energies\n    energies = []\n    for i in range(num_frames):\n        frame = waveform[0, i * frame_size : (i + 1) * frame_size]\n        energy = torch.sqrt(torch.mean(frame ** 2)).item()\n        energies.append(energy)\n\n    # Normalize energies\n    energies = np.array(energies)\n    if energies.max() > 0:\n        energies = energies / energies.max()\n\n    # Find speech frames\n    speech_frames = energies > energy_threshold\n\n    # Convert to segments (merge adjacent frames)\n    segments = []\n    in_speech = False\n    start_frame = 0\n\n    for i, is_speech in enumerate(speech_frames):\n        if is_speech and not in_speech:\n            start_frame = i\n            in_speech = True\n        elif not is_speech and in_speech:\n            if i - start_frame >= 3:  # Minimum 3 frames (~90ms)\n                segments.append({\n                    'start': start_frame * frame_duration,\n                    'end': i * frame_duration\n                })\n            in_speech = False\n\n    if in_speech:\n        segments.append({\n            'start': start_frame * frame_duration,\n            'end': num_frames * frame_duration\n        })\n\n    # Merge nearby segments\n    merged = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        if merged and seg['start'] - merged[-1]['end'] < 0.3:  # Gap < 300ms\n            merged[-1]['end'] = seg['end']\n        else:\n            merged.append(seg)\n\n    return merged\n\n\ndef extract_speaker_embeddings(waveform, sr, segments, speaker_model, min_duration=0.5):\n    \"\"\"Extract speaker embeddings for each segment.\"\"\"\n    embeddings = []\n    valid_segments = []\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        duration = seg['end'] - seg['start']\n        if duration < min_duration:\n            continue\n\n        start_sample = int(seg['start'] * sr)\n        end_sample = int(seg['end'] * sr)\n        segment_audio = waveform[:, start_sample:end_sample]\n\n        # Ensure minimum length for embedding\n        if segment_audio.shape[1] < sr * 0.5:\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            embedding = speaker_model.encode_batch(segment_audio)\n            embeddings.append(embedding.squeeze().cpu().numpy())\n            valid_segments.append(seg)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Skipping segment {seg['start']:.2f}-{seg['end']:.2f}: {e}\")\n\n    return np.array(embeddings) if embeddings else None, valid_segments\n\n\ndef cluster_speakers(embeddings, num_speakers=None, threshold=0.7):\n    \"\"\"Cluster speaker embeddings using hierarchical clustering.\"\"\"\n    if embeddings is None or len(embeddings) < 2:\n        return [0] * len(embeddings) if embeddings is not None else []\n\n    # Compute pairwise distances\n    distances = pdist(embeddings, metric='cosine')\n\n    # Hierarchical clustering\n    linkage_matrix = linkage(distances, method='average')\n\n    if num_speakers:\n        labels = fcluster(linkage_matrix, num_speakers, criterion='maxclust')\n    else:\n        labels = fcluster(linkage_matrix, threshold, criterion='distance')\n\n    # Convert to 0-indexed\n    return [l - 1 for l in labels]\n\n\ndef transcribe_with_whisper(audio_path, model_name=\"base\"):\n    \"\"\"Transcribe audio using Whisper with word timestamps.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading Whisper model '{model_name}'...\")\n    model = whisper.load_model(model_name)\n\n    print(\"Transcribing audio...\")\n    result = model.transcribe(\n        audio_path,\n        word_timestamps=True,\n        fp16=False,  # CPU mode\n        verbose=False\n    )\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef align_transcription_with_speakers(whisper_result, speaker_segments):\n    \"\"\"Align Whisper transcription segments with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n    aligned = []\n\n    for segment in whisper_result[\"segments\"]:\n        seg_start = segment[\"start\"]\n        seg_end = segment[\"end\"]\n        seg_mid = (seg_start + seg_end) / 2\n\n        # Find speaker at segment midpoint\n        speaker = None\n        for spk_seg in speaker_segments:\n            if spk_seg['start'] <= seg_mid <= spk_seg['end']:\n                speaker = spk_seg['speaker']\n                break\n\n        if speaker is None:\n            # Find closest speaker segment\n            min_dist = float('inf')\n            for spk_seg in speaker_segments:\n                dist = min(abs(seg_mid - spk_seg['start']), abs(seg_mid - spk_seg['end']))\n                if dist < min_dist:\n                    min_dist = dist\n                    speaker = spk_seg['speaker']\n\n        aligned.append({\n            \"start\": seg_start,\n            \"end\": seg_end,\n            \"text\": segment[\"text\"].strip(),\n            \"speaker\": speaker or \"SPEAKER_00\"\n        })\n\n    return aligned\n\n\ndef write_rttm(speaker_segments, output_path, file_id=\"input\"):\n    \"\"\"Write seg['start']\n            # Convert SPEAKER_XX to spkXX format\n            spk = seg['speaker']\n            if spk.startswith(\"SPEAKER_\"):\n                num = int(spk.split(\"_\")[1])\n                spk_id = f\"spk{num:02d}\"\n            else:\n                spk_id = spk\n\n            f.write(f\"SPEAKER {file_id} 1 {start:.6f} {duration:.6f} <NA> <NA> {spk_id} <NA> <NA>\\n\")\n\n\ndef write_ass_subtitles(aligned_segments, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Write ASS subtitle file with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n    header = \"\"\"[Script Info]\nScriptType: v4.00+\nPlayResX: 1920\nPlayResY: 1080\nTitle: Speaker Diarization Subtitles\n\n[V4+ Styles]\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\nStyle: Default,Arial,20,&H00FFFFFF,&H000000FF,&H00000000,&H00000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,2,2,2,10,10,10,1\n\n[Events]\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(header)\n        for seg in aligned_segments:\n            start = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['start'])\n            end = seconds_to_ass_time(seg['end'])\n            speaker = seg['speaker']\n            text = seg['text'].replace('\\n', '\\\\N')  # ASS newline escape\n\n            f.write(f\"Dialogue: 0,{start},{end},Default,,0,0,0,,{speaker}: {text}\\n\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    audio_path = \"/root/audio.wav\"\n    rttm_path = \"/root/diarization.rttm\"\n    ass_path = \"/root/subtitles.ass\"\n
